### 1136. [ceremony](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ceremony) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈserəməni/` · **US:** `/ˈserəməʊni/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cer/cerem/ceremony__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cer/cerem/ceremony__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवसर, अतिशयोक्ति, विधि
- **Examples:**
  - More than 1 000 people attended the ceremony.
  - an awards/opening ceremony
  - a wedding/marriage/graduation ceremony
  - a religious/civil ceremony

### 1137. [chain](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/chain_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/tʃeɪn/` · **US:** `/tʃeɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chain/chain__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chain/chain__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बंधन, क्रम, छल्ला
- **Examples:**
  - She wore a heavy gold chain around her neck.
  - The mayor wore his chain of office.
  - a bicycle chain
  - in chains The prisoners were kept in chains (= with chains around their arms and legs, to prevent them from escaping).

### 1138. [chair](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/chair_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/tʃeə(r)/` · **US:** `/tʃer/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chair/chair__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chair/chair__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गायक  दल का स्थानअ, कुर्सी, अध्यक्ष
- **Examples:**
  - a table and chairs
  - on a chair Sit on your chair!
  - in a chair an old man asleep in a chair (= an armchair)
  - She was sitting in her favourite chair.

### 1139. [challenge](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/challenge_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtʃælɪndʒ/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃælɪndʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chall/challenge__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chall/challenge__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ललकार, दावा, आपत्ति
- **Examples:**
  - a tough/major/significant challenge
  - The role will be the biggest challenge of his acting career.
  - She still faces many challenges (= has to deal with them).
  - Destruction of the environment is one of the most serious challenges we face.

### 1140. [champion](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/champion_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtʃæmpiən/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃæmpiən/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/champ/champion__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/champ/champion__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सर्वोत्तम, लड़ने  वाला, शूरवीर
- **Examples:**
  - the world/European/national/Olympic champion
  - the defending champion (= the person who was champion the last time and is trying to be champion again)
  - the reigning champion (= the person who is champion now)
  - the world basketball champions

### 1141. [channel](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/channel_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtʃænl/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃænl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chann/channel__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chann/channel__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नहर, मार्ग, माध्यम
- **Examples:**
  - a television/TV channel
  - cable/digital/satellite channels
  - a news channel
  - a music/sports/movie channel

### 1142. [chapter](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/chapter) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtʃæptə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃæptər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chapt/chapter__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chapt/chapter__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सभा, याजक सभा, अध्याय
- **Examples:**
  - to read/write a chapter
  - He devotes an entire chapter to this topic.
  - I've just finished Chapter 3.
  - I read chapter after chapter of the book.

### 1143. [characteristic](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/characteristic_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˌkærəktəˈrɪstɪk/` · **US:** `/ˌkærəktəˈrɪstɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chara/characteristic__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chara/characteristic__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विशेष
- **Examples:**
  - characteristic of something/somebody Community support of families is characteristic of many societies.
  - This is just one example of the rudeness that was so characteristic of him.
  - the characteristic features of his style of painting
  - She spoke with characteristic enthusiasm.

### 1144. [characterize](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/characterize) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkærəktəraɪz/` · **US:** `/ˈkærəktəraɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chara/characterize__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chara/characterize__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** की विशेषता होना, चरित्र चित्रण करना, विशेषता होना
- **Examples:**
  - the rolling hills that characterize this part of England
  - The city is characterized by tall modern buildings in steel and glass.

### 1145. [charge](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/charge_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/tʃɑːdʒ/` · **US:** `/tʃɑːrdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/charg/charge__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/charg/charge__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उत्तरदायी, दाम, पद
- **Examples:**
  - admission charges
  - charge for something We have to make a small charge for refreshments.
  - Delivery is free of charge.

### 1146. [charity](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/charity) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtʃærəti/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃærəti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chari/charity__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chari/charity__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उदारता, दया, दान
- **Examples:**
  - a registered charity
  - the UK's largest children's charity
  - Many charities sent money to help the victims of the famine.
  - The concert will raise money for local charities.

### 1147. [chart](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/chart_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/tʃɑːt/` · **US:** `/tʃɑːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chart/chart__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chart/chart__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तालिका, समुद्री नक्शा, रेखाचिट्र
- **Examples:**
  - Some shares have lost two-thirds of their value since being issued (see chart).
  - chart of somebody/something The charts of 138 patients with chronic heart failure were reviewed.
  - The accompanying chart shows data for eight countries.
  - a sales chart (= showing the level of a company’s sales)

### 1148. [chase](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/chase_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/tʃeɪs/` · **US:** `/tʃeɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chase/chase__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chase/chase__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आखेट, लंबी  दौड़, शिकार
- **Examples:**
  - The thieves were caught by police after a short chase.
  - a high-speed car chase
  - We lost him in the narrow streets and had to give up the chase (= stop chasing him).
  - to take up the chase (= start chasing somebody)

### 1149. [cheap](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/cheap_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/tʃiːp/` · **US:** `/tʃiːp/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/che/cheap/cheap__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/che/cheap/cheap__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घटिया, सस्ता, अनुदार
- **Examples:**
  - cheap imports/flights
  - Their cheap prices have helped them pick up new customers.
  - They're offering incredibly cheap fares to Eastern Europe.
  - Cycling is a cheap way to get around.

### 1150. [cheer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/cheer_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/tʃɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/tʃɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/che/cheer/cheer__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/che/cheer/cheer__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भोजन, हर्ष, मनोदशा
- **Examples:**
  - A great cheer went up from the crowd.
  - cheers of encouragement
  - Three cheers for the winners! (= used when you are asking a group of people to cheer three times, in order to congratulate somebody, etc.)

### 1151. [chemical](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/chemical_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkemɪkl/` · **US:** `/ˈkemɪkl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/che/chemi/chemical__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/che/chemi/chemical__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रसायनिक
- **Examples:**
  - changes in the chemical composition of the atmosphere

### 1152. [chief](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/chief_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/tʃiːf/` · **US:** `/tʃiːf/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/chi/chief/chief__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/chi/chief/chief__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मालिक, प्रमुख, मुख्य
- **Examples:**
  - the chief cause/problem/reason
  - He became the chief architect of the Treaty of Paris.
  - She cites the economy as her chief concern.
  - one of the President’s chief rivals

### 1153. [childhood](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/childhood) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtʃaɪldhʊd/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃaɪldhʊd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/chi/child/childhood__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/chi/child/childhood__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बाल्यावस्था, बचपन
- **Examples:**
  - childhood, adolescence, and adulthood
  - He spent his childhood in India.
  - She had a happy childhood.
  - the problem of childhood obesity

### 1154. [circumstance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/circumstance) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɜːkəmstæns/` · **US:** `/ˈsɜːrkəmstæns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cir/circu/circumstance__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cir/circu/circumstance__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दशा/अवस्था, स्थिति, परिस्थिति
- **Examples:**
  - Police said there were no suspicious circumstances surrounding the boy's death.
  - under … circumstances Under normal circumstances, your white blood cells are able to fight infections.
  - in … circumstances The company reserves the right to cancel this agreement in certain circumstances.
  - In exceptional circumstances, detainees could be denied access to a lawyer.

### 1155. [cite](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/cite) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/saɪt/` · **US:** `/saɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cit/cite_/cite__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cit/cite_/cite__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तलब  करना, हवाला देना, उल्लेख करना
- **Examples:**
  - cite something She cited examples of companies the city has helped relocate or expand.
  - Citing unnamed sources, the newspaper reports that the two companies are nearing a deal.
  - There is no reason to doubt the information in the articles cited above.
  - He cited personal reasons for his decision to resign.

### 1156. [citizen](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/citizen) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɪtɪzn/` · **US:** `/ˈsɪtɪzn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cit/citiz/citizen__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cit/citiz/citizen__us_7.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्थानिक, नागरिक, निवासी
- **Examples:**
  - The defeat of the president did not change the lives of ordinary citizens for the better.
  - an economic slowdown that has left millions of our fellow citizens unemployed
  - She's Italian by birth but is now an Australian citizen.
  - Polish citizens living in other parts of the European Union

### 1157. [civil](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/civil) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɪvl/` · **US:** `/ˈsɪvl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/civ/civil/civil__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/civ/civil/civil__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सभ्य, नम्र, नागरिक
- **Examples:**
  - civil unrest (= that is caused by groups of people within a country)
  - civil strife/conflict
  - There were concerns that the economic reforms might lead to civil unrest.

### 1158. [clarify](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/clarify) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈklærəfaɪ/` · **US:** `/ˈklærəfaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cla/clari/clarify__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cla/clari/clarify__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शुद्ध करना, स्पष्टीकरण देना, स्पष्ट करना
- **Examples:**
  - clarify something to clarify a situation/problem/issue
  - I hope this clarifies my position.
  - The law on drugs needs to be clarified.
  - Talking to someone has helped clarify my feelings.

### 1159. [classic](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/classic_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈklæsɪk/` · **US:** `/ˈklæsɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cla/class/classic__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cla/class/classic__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उत्कृष्ट, अनलंकृत, आदर्श
- **Examples:**
  - a classic film/story/car/game
  - I grew up listening to classic rock.
  - a classic novel/study/goal
  - This classic novel was first published in 1938.

### 1160. [client](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/client) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈklaɪənt/` · **US:** `/ˈklaɪənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cli/clien/client__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cli/clien/client__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उपभोगता, ग्राहक, असामी
- **Examples:**
  - She's a well-known lawyer with many famous clients.
  - potential/prospective clients
  - It is our job to act on behalf of the client.
  - We will advise the client on the best way to solve the problem.

### 1161. [climate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/climate) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈklaɪmət/` · **US:** `/ˈklaɪmət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cli/clima/climate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cli/clima/climate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जलवायु, वातावरण, आबोहवा
- **Examples:**

  - the harsh climate of the Arctic regions

### 1162. [clinic](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/clinic) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈklɪnɪk/` · **US:** `/ˈklɪnɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cli/clini/clinic__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cli/clini/clinic__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** क्लीनिक, चिकित्सालय
- **Examples:**
  - Your local family planning clinic can give you advice about birth control.

### 1163. [clerk](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/clerk_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/klɑːk/` · **US:** `/klɜːrk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cle/clerk/clerk__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cle/clerk/clerk__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पादरी, विद्वान, पेशकार
- **Examples:**
  - The clerk at the counter gave me too little change.
  - The sales clerk answered all our questions.

### 1164. [coach](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/coach_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kəʊtʃ/` · **US:** `/kəʊtʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/coa/coach/coach__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/coa/coach/coach__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शिक्षक, प्राइवेट ट्यूटर, रेल का  डिब्बा
- **Examples:**

  - a basketball/football coach
  - the team coach
  - the England coach

### 1165. [coal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/coal) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kəʊl/` · **US:** `/kəʊl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/coa/coal_/coal__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/coa/coal_/coal__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अंगार, कोयला
- **Examples:**
  - I put more coal on the fire.
  - a lump of coal
  - coal mining
  - She sat by the coal fire in the kitchen.

### 1166. [coalition](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/coalition) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌkəʊəˈlɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌkəʊəˈlɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/coa/coali/coalition__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/coa/coali/coalition__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अस्थायी संगठन्, संगठन, मिला  जुला

  - a two-party coalition
  - a coalition government
  - (in) coalition (with somebody) The two parties governed in coalition for four years.

### 1167. [code](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/code_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kəʊd/` · **US:** `/kəʊd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cod/code_/code__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cod/code_/code__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नियमावली, गुप्त भाषा, सांकेतिक अंक

  - to break/crack a code (= to understand and read the message)

  - In the event of the machine not operating correctly, an error code will appear.
  - Tap your code number into the machine.

### 1168. [coffee](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/coffee) *(noun)*

### 1169. [cognitive](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/cognitive) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒɡnətɪv/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːɡnətɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cog/cogni/cognitive__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cog/cogni/cognitive__us_1.mp3)

### 1170. [collapse](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/collapse_2) *(noun)*

### 1175. [column](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/column) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒləm/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːləm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/col/colum/column__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/col/colum/column__us_1.mp3)

### 1176. [combine](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/combine_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kəmˈbaɪn/` · **US:** `/kəmˈbaɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/combi/combine__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/combi/combine__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मिलना[मिलाना], जत्था, मिलाना
- **Examples:**
  - combine to do something Hydrogen and oxygen combine to form water.
  - Several factors had combined to ruin our plans.
  - combine with something (to do something) Hydrogen combines with oxygen to form water.
  - Archaeological and historical evidence combine to create a picture of what life must have been like at that time.

### 1177. [comfort](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/comfort_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkʌmfət/` · **US:** `/ˈkʌmfərt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/comfo/comfort__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/comfo/comfort__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आराम, सुविधा, सुख
- **Examples:**
  - The hotel offers a high standard of comfort and service.
  - I didn't want to leave the comfort of my bed.
  - in comfort They had enough money to live in comfort in their old age.
  - in the comfort of something Watch the latest movies in the comfort of your own home.

### 1178. [comfortable](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/comfortable) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkʌmfətəbl/` · **US:** `/ˈkʌmfərtəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/comfo/comfortable__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/comfo/comfortable__us_4.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - It's such a comfortable bed.
  - These new shoes are not very comfortable.
  - clothes that are comfortable to wear
  - a warm comfortable house

### 1179. [command](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/command_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kəˈmɑːnd/` · **US:** `/kəˈmænd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/comma/command__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/comma/command__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संकेत, शासन, कमान
- **Examples:**
  - Begin when I give the command.
  - You must obey the captain's commands.
  - command to do something He issued the command to retreat.
  - Police said he ignored their commands to stop.

### 1180. [comment](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/comment_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒment/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːment/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/comme/comment__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/comme/comment__us_1.mp3)

  - snide/critical/derogatory comments
  - comment about something Have you any comment to make about the cause of the disaster?

### 1181. [commercial](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/commercial_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kəˈmɜːʃl/` · **US:** `/kəˈmɜːrʃl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/comme/commercial__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/comme/commercial__us_2.mp3)

### 1182. [commission](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/commission_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kəˈmɪʃn/` · **US:** `/kəˈmɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/commi/commission__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/commi/commission__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अधिकार, कमीशन, दलाली
- **Examples:**

  - (British English) The government has set up a commission of inquiry into the disturbances at the prison.

### 1183. [commit](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/commit) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kəˈmɪt/` · **US:** `/kəˈmɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/commi/commit__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/commi/commit__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - to commit a crime/an offence
  - to commit murder/fraud/adultery
  - to commit an act of violence/terrorism
  - to commit an error/a sin

### 1184. [commitment](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/commitment) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kəˈmɪtmənt/` · **US:** `/kəˈmɪtmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/commi/commitment__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/commi/commitment__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुपुर्दगी, वादा, वचनबद्धता
- **Examples:**
  - I'm not ready to make a long-term commitment.
  - They have failed to honour their commitments.
  - commitment to somebody/something The company has shown a commitment to diversity over the years.
  - He questioned the government's commitment to public services.

### 1185. [committee](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/committee) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kəˈmɪti/` · **US:** `/kəˈmɪti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/commi/committee__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/commi/committee__us_2.mp3)

  - The committee has/have decided to close the restaurant.
  - The committee unanimously approved the plan.

### 1186. [communicate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/communicate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kəˈmjuːnɪkeɪt/` · **US:** `/kəˈmjuːnɪkeɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/commu/communicate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/commu/communicate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** फैलाना, व्यक्त करना, पहुँचाना
- **Examples:**
  - We only communicate by email.
  - They communicated in sign language.
  - Candidates must be able to communicate effectively.
  - communicate with somebody/something Dolphins use sound to communicate with each other.

### 1187. [communication](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/communication) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kəˌmjuːnɪˈkeɪʃn/` · **US:** `/kəˌmjuːnɪˈkeɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/commu/communication__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/commu/communication__us_2.mp3)

  - communication between A and B Good communication between team leaders and members is essential.

  - The campaign will appear across all communication channels.
  - helping students develop their communication skills

### 1188. [compete](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/compete) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kəmˈpiːt/` · **US:** `/kəmˈpiːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/compe/compete__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/compe/compete__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - The games were an outstanding success, with almost 2 400 athletes competing.
  - compete in something He's hoping to compete in the London marathon.
  - Only 21 bodybuilders competed in the event.
  - compete against somebody They are competing against teams of full-time professional players.

### 1189. [competition](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/competition) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌkɒmpəˈtɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌkɑːmpəˈtɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/compe/competition__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/compe/competition__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - a design/an art/a talent competition

  - Hundreds of schools entered the competition.
  - She's won a prestigious singing competition.

### 1190. [competitive](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/competitive) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kəmˈpetətɪv/` · **US:** `/kəmˈpetətɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/compe/competitive__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/compe/competitive__us_3.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - competitive games/sports
  - Graduates have to fight for jobs in an increasingly competitive market.
  - Those businesses are operating in an extremely competitive environment.
  - The company has succeeded in a highly competitive industry.

### 1191. [compile](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/compile) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kəmˈpaɪl/` · **US:** `/kəmˈpaɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/compi/compile__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/compi/compile__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जमा करना, संग्रह करना, इकट्ठा करना
- **Examples:**
  - We are trying to compile a list of suitable people for the job.
  - The album was compiled from live recordings from last year's tour.
  - The figures were compiled from a survey of 2  000 schoolchildren.

### 1192. [complain](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/complain) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kəmˈpleɪn/` · **US:** `/kəmˈpleɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/compl/complain__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/compl/complain__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शिकायत करना, शिकायत  करना, दुखड़ा रोना
- **Examples:**
  - She never complains, but she's obviously exhausted.
  - (informal) ‘How are you?’ ‘Oh, I can't complain (= I'm all right).’
  - complain to somebody Annoyed residents have complained to the police.
  - complain about something Shoppers complained about the lack of toilet facilities.

### 1193. [complaint](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/complaint) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kəmˈpleɪnt/` · **US:** `/kəmˈpleɪnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/compl/complaint__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/compl/complaint__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शिकायत, झगड़ा, रोग
- **Examples:**
  - a formal complaint
  - She said the police had immediately investigated the complaint.
  - complaint about somebody/something I'd like to make a complaint about the noise.
  - (formal) to file/lodge (= make) a complaint

### 1194. [complete](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/complete_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kəmˈpliːt/` · **US:** `/kəmˈpliːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/compl/complete__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/compl/complete__us_1.mp3)

### 1200. [comprise](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/comprise) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kəmˈpraɪz/` · **US:** `/kəmˈpraɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/compr/comprise__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/compr/comprise__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समाविष्ट करना, सम्मिलित करना, बने हुए होना
- **Examples:**
  - comprise something The collection comprises 327 paintings.
  - a DVD box set comprising seasons 4 and 5
  - be comprised of somebody/something The committee is comprised of representatives from both the public and private sectors.

## Words 1,201 – 1,300

### 1201. [concentrate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/concentrate_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒnsntreɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnsntreɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconcentrate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconcentrate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ध्यान  केन्द्रित करना, ध्यान केन्द्रित करना, ध्यान देना
- **Examples:**
  - I can't concentrate with all that noise going on.
  - concentrate on something I struggled to concentrate on my job because I was worried about my son.
  - concentrate on doing something She tried to concentrate on reading her book but couldn't.
  - concentrate something Nothing concentrates the mind better than the knowledge that you could die tomorrow (= it makes you think very clearly).

### 1202. [concept](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/concept) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒnsept/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnsept/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconcept__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconcept__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रत्यय, अवधारणा
- **Examples:**
  - concept of something the concept of social class
  - helping students grasp abstract concepts such as leadership and teamwork
  - basic/fundamental/key/core concepts
  - concept that… the concept that everyone should have equality of opportunity

### 1203. [conclude](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/conclude) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈkluːd/` · **US:** `/kənˈkluːd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconclude__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconclude__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निश्चित करना, तय करना, समाप्त होना
- **Examples:**
  - conclude something It is too early for us to conclude anything.
  - conclude something from something What do you conclude from that?
  - conclude (that)… The report concluded (that) the cheapest option was to close the laboratory.
  - conclude from something that… He concluded from their remarks that they were not in favour of the plan.

### 1204. [conclusion](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/conclusion) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈkluːʒn/` · **US:** `/kənˈkluːʒn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconclusion__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconclusion__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समाप्ति, निष्कर्ष, अंतिम निर्णय
- **Examples:**
  - We can safely draw some conclusions from our discussion.
  - He arrived at a different conclusion.
  - There is abundant evidence to support this conclusion.
  - Other studies have yielded the same conclusion.

### 1205. [concrete](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/concrete_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒŋkriːt/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnkriːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconcrete__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconcrete__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** साकार, पदार्थ रूप में
- **Examples:**
  - a concrete floor

### 1206. [conduct](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/conduct_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒndʌkt/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːndʌkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcond/xconduct__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcond/xconduct__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आचरण, प्रणाली, बर्ताव
- **Examples:**
  - The sport has a strict code of conduct.
  - improving standards of training and professional conduct
  - Any allegation of criminal conduct has to be taken seriously.
  - a complaint about the conduct of a police officer

### 1207. [conference](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/conference) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒnfərəns/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnfərəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconference__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconference__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सम्मेलन, अधिवेशन
- **Examples:**
  - The hotel is used for exhibitions, conferences and social events.
  - to host/organize a conference
  - to speak at/address a conference
  - The conference will be held in Glasgow.

### 1208. [confidence](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/confidence) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒnfɪdəns/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnfɪdəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfidence__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfidence__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भरोसा, दृढ विश्वास, दृढ  विश्वास
- **Examples:**
  - The president's actions hardly inspire confidence.
  - confidence in somebody/something The players all have confidence in their manager.
  - He believes his reforms will restore confidence in the financial markets.
  - The survey revealed a lack of confidence in the government.

### 1209. [confident](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/confident) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒnfɪdənt/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnfɪdənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfident__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfident__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निडर, कभी न  चुकने  वाला, विश्वासपूर्ण
- **Examples:**
  - She was in a relaxed, confident mood.
  - Beneath his confident and charming exterior, lurked a mass of insecurities.
  - confident about something I was actually fairly confident about my chances.
  - confident about doing something The teacher wants the children to feel confident about asking questions when they don't understand.

### 1210. [confirm](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/confirm) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈfɜːm/` · **US:** `/kənˈfɜːrm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfirm__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfirm__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निश्चित करना, पक्का करना, पक्का ईसाई बनाना
- **Examples:**
  - confirm something His guilty expression confirmed my suspicions.
  - to confirm a diagnosis/report
  - to confirm results/findings
  - Rumours of job losses were later confirmed.

### 1211. [conflict](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/conflict_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒnflɪkt/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnflɪkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconflict__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconflict__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संघर्ष, विरोध/संघर्ष, द्वंद्व
- **Examples:**
  - The violence was the result of political conflicts.
  - conflict between A and B (over something) a conflict between two cultures
  - His task will be to resolve potential conflicts between the British and Scottish governments.
  - conflict with somebody The prime minister wants to avoid conflict with the unions.

### 1212. [confront](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/confront) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈfrʌnt/` · **US:** `/kənˈfrʌnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfront__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfront__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सामना होना, सामना, सामना करना
- **Examples:**
  - What is to be done about the economic problems confronting the country?
  - I was suddenly confronted by the task of rewriting the entire book.
  - The government found itself confronted by massive opposition.

### 1213. [confuse](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/confuse) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈfjuːz/` · **US:** `/kənˈfjuːz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfuse__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfuse__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मिलाना, उलझा देना, गड़बड़ा देना
- **Examples:**
  - confuse somebody These two sets of statistics are guaranteed to confuse the public.
  - confuse somebody with something They confused me with conflicting accounts of what happened.

### 1214. [confusion](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/confusion) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈfjuːʒn/` · **US:** `/kənˈfjuːʒn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfusion__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconf/xconfusion__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अस्तव्यस्तता, संभ्रम, भान्ति
- **Examples:**
  - The announcement caused a lot of confusion.
  - confusion about/over something There is some confusion about what the correct procedure should be.
  - confusion as to something There was a confusion as to what to do next.

### 1215. [connect](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/connect) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kəˈnekt/` · **US:** `/kəˈnekt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconn/xconnect__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconn/xconnect__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मिलाना, जुड़ा  होना, लगना
- **Examples:**
  - connect A and B The towns are connected by train and bus services.
  - connect A to B The island is connected to the mainland by a bridge.
  - connect A with B The canal was built to connect Sheffield with the Humber estuary.
  - The rooms on this floor connect.

### 1216. [connection](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/connection) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kəˈnekʃn/` · **US:** `/kəˈnekʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconn/xconnection__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconn/xconnection__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नाता, संबन्ध, संपर्क
- **Examples:**
  - connection between A and B Scientists have established a connection between cholesterol levels and heart disease.
  - connection with something a direct/close/strong connection with something
  - His resignation must have some connection with the recent scandal.
  - connection to something Consumers are more likely to buy something from a person or shop they feel a personal connection to.

### 1217. [conscious](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/conscious) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒnʃəs/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnʃəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconscious__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconscious__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सतर्क, जागरूक, साभिप्राय
- **Examples:**
  - conscious of something She's very conscious of the problems involved.
  - I am conscious of the fact that there's a limit to what we can achieve here.
  - conscious of doing something He became acutely conscious of having failed his parents.
  - conscious that… I was vaguely conscious that I was being watched.

### 1218. [consciousness](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consciousness) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒnʃəsnəs/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnʃəsnəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsciousness__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsciousness__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जानकारी, होश, चेतना , होश
- **Examples:**
  - I can't remember any more—I must have lost consciousness.
  - She did not regain consciousness and died the next day.

### 1219. [consensus](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consensus) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsensəs/` · **US:** `/kənˈsensəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsensus__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsensus__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सहमति, तालमेल, अनुकूलता
- **Examples:**
  - consensus (about/on something) She is skilled at achieving consensus on sensitive issues.
  - There is a growing consensus of opinion on this issue.
  - an attempt to reach a consensus
  - There now exists a broad political consensus in favour of economic reform.

### 1220. [consent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consent_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsent/` · **US:** `/kənˈsent/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsent__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वीकृति, सहमति, अनुमति
- **Examples:**
  - The written consent of a parent is required.
  - consent to something Children under 16 cannot give consent to medical treatment.
  - to refuse/withhold your consent
  - without (somebody's) consent He is charged with taking a car without the owner's consent.

### 1221. [consequence](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consequence) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒnsɪkwəns/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnsɪkwens/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsequence__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsequence__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** परिणाम, नतीजा, सामाजिक  प्रतिष्ठा
- **Examples:**
  - Remember that actions have consequences.
  - to suffer/face the consequences of something
  - dire/disastrous/negative/adverse consequences
  - Have you considered the possible consequences?

### 1222. [conservative](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/conservative_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsɜːvətɪv/` · **US:** `/kənˈsɜːrvətɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconservative__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconservative__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रूढ़िवादी
- **Examples:**
  - the conservative views of his parents
  - music which is accessible to an audience with extremely conservative tastes
  - The southern state's inhabitants tend to be socially conservative.
  - Her style of dress was never conservative.

### 1223. [considerable](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/considerable) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsɪdərəbl/` · **US:** `/kənˈsɪdərəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsiderable__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsiderable__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बहुत अधिक, महत्वपूर्ण, बहुत
- **Examples:**
  - The project wasted a considerable amount of time and money.
  - Damage to the building was considerable.
  - Considerable progress has been made in finding a cure for the disease.

### 1224. [consideration](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consideration) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˌsɪdəˈreɪʃn/` · **US:** `/kənˌsɪdəˈreɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsideration__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsideration__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ध्यान, विचार, मुआवजा
- **Examples:**
  - An employer is legally bound to give due consideration to the request.
  - Careful consideration should be given to issues of health and safety.
  - Her ideas are worthy of serious consideration.
  - to merit/deserve/warrant further consideration

### 1225. [consist](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consist) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsɪst/` · **US:** `/kənˈsɪst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsist__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsist__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, शामिल होना, युक्त हो

### 1226. [consistent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consistent) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsɪstənt/` · **US:** `/kənˈsɪstənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsistent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsistent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुरूप, अटल, एक सा
- **Examples:**
  - a consistent approach to the problem
  - a consistent pattern of behaviour
  - He has been Milan's most consistent player (= who plays well most often) this season.
  - consistent in something She's not very consistent in the way she treats her children.

### 1227. [constant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/constant_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒnstənt/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnstənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconstant__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconstant__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अपरिवर्तनशील
- **Examples:**
  - There were constant interruptions.
  - a constant stream of visitors all day
  - The ruined buildings serve as a constant reminder of the war.
  - The constant threat of violence from the other inmates was hard to deal with.

### 1228. [constitute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/constitute) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒnstɪtjuːt/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːnstɪtuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconstitute__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconstitute__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, बनाना, निर्माण करना
- **Examples:**
  - Does such an activity constitute a criminal offence?
  - Climate change constitutes a major threat to life.
  - to constitute a danger/crime/breach/nuisance
  - His action was interpreted as constituting a threat to the community.

### 1229. [construct](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/construct_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈstrʌkt/` · **US:** `/kənˈstrʌkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconstruct__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconstruct__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बनाना, रचना, खींचना
- **Examples:**
  - construct something The building was constructed in 1993.
  - construct something out of something They constructed a shelter out of fallen branches.
  - construct something from something The frame is constructed from lightweight aluminium.
  - construct something of something On the smaller islands, houses are often constructed of local materials.

### 1230. [construction](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/construction) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈstrʌkʃn/` · **US:** `/kənˈstrʌkʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconstruction__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconstruction__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निर्माण, अभिप्राय, निर्माण कार्य
- **Examples:**
  - Construction began this year and will take approximately 18 months.
  - the costs of road construction and maintenance
  - Work has begun on the construction of the new airport.
  - the construction of a new database

### 1231. [consult](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consult) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsʌlt/` · **US:** `/kənˈsʌlt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsult__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsult__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विचार विमर्श करना, राय  लेना, राय लेना
- **Examples:**
  - consult somebody If the pain continues, consult your doctor.
  - consult somebody about something Have you consulted your lawyer about this?
  - a consulting engineer (= one who has expert knowledge and gives advice)
  - (North American English) consult with somebody (about/on something) Consult with your physician about possible treatments.

### 1232. [consume](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consume) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsjuːm/` · **US:** `/kənˈsuːm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsume__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsume__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** खाना, जलाना, काम में लाना/व्यय करना
- **Examples:**
  - The electricity industry consumes large amounts of fossil fuels.
  - 25 per cent of the world’s population consumes 80 per cent of the planet’s resources.

### 1233. [consumer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consumer) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsjuːmə(r)/` · **US:** `/kənˈsuːmər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsumer__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsumer__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उपभोगता, उपभोक्ता
- **Examples:**
  - consumer spending/demand
  - links between producers and consumers
  - Health-conscious consumers want more information about the food they buy.
  - a consumer society (= one where buying and selling is considered to be very important)

### 1234. [consumption](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consumption) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsʌmpʃn/` · **US:** `/kənˈsʌmpʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsumption__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsumption__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** खपत, संयोग, फुप्फुसीय यक्षमा
- **Examples:**
  - the production of fuel for domestic consumption (= to be used in the country where it is produced)
  - Gas and oil consumption always increases in cold weather.
  - The meat was declared unfit for human consumption.
  - He was advised to reduce his alcohol consumption.

### 1235. [contact](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/contact_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒntækt/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːntækt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontact__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontact__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सम्पर्क, सम्बन्ध, रोग  फैलानेवाला व्यक्ति
- **Examples:**
  - contact with somebody I don't have much contact with my uncle.
  - We've been unable to establish radio contact with the pilot.
  - She's lost contact with (= no longer sees or writes to) her son.
  - I finally made contact with (= succeeded in speaking to or meeting) her in Paris.

### 1236. [contain](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/contain) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈteɪn/` · **US:** `/kənˈteɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontain__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontain__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, रोकना, घेरना
- **Examples:**
  - This drink doesn't contain any alcohol.
  - The documents contain sensitive information.
  - Electronic products contain hazardous materials that cannot be deposited in landfills.
  - His account contained an element of truth.

### 1237. [container](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/container) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈteɪnə(r)/` · **US:** `/kənˈteɪnər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontainer__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontainer__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आधान, पात्र, धारक/पात्र
- **Examples:**
  - a plastic container
  - in a container Food will last longer if kept in an airtight container.
  - container of something These women carry heavy containers of water over long distances.
  - container for something containers for nuclear materials

### 1238. [contemporary](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/contemporary_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈtemprəri/` · **US:** `/kənˈtempəreri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontemporary__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontemporary__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समकालीन
- **Examples:**
  - We have no contemporary account of the battle (= written near the time that it happened).
  - contemporary with somebody/something He was contemporary with the dramatist Congreve.
  - The amphitheatre is roughly contemporary with the Colosseum in Rome.

### 1239. [contest](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/contest_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒntest/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːntest/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontest__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontest__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रतियोगिता, संघर्ष
- **Examples:**
  - to hold a singing/talent contest
  - to enter/win a contest
  - in a contest She was awarded the first prize in an essay contest .
  - contest between A and B It was a close contest between two evenly matched crews.

### 1240. [context](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/context) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒntekst/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːntekst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontext__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontext__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** परिस्थिति, प्रसंग, सन्दर्भ
- **Examples:**
  - Such databases are being used in a wide range of contexts.
  - to examine the wider/broader context of the war
  - the historical/cultural/social context
  - The government must consider the context of the protests.

### 1241. [continuous](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/continuous) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈtɪnjuəs/` · **US:** `/kənˈtɪnjuəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontinuous__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontinuous__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सतत, लगातार, लागातार
- **Examples:**
  - Recovery after the accident will be a continuous process that may take several months.
  - The organization aims to create a culture of continuous improvement .
  - a continuous stream of data
  - a continuous flow of knowledge, information and understanding

### 1242. [contract](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/contract_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒntrækt/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːntrækt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontract__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontract__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ठेका, सगाई, संविदा
- **Examples:**
  - All employees have a written contract of employment.
  - a research/modelling/recording contract
  - a lucrative government contract
  - contract with somebody to enter into/negotiate/sign a contract with the supplier

### 1243. [contrast](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/contrast_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒntrɑːst/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːntræst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontrast__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontrast__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विरोध, वैषम्य
- **Examples:**
  - The two cities make an interesting contrast.
  - contrast between A and B There is an obvious contrast between the cultures of East and West.
  - in contrast In contrast, the south suffered very little hurricane damage.
  - in contrast to somebody/something The situation when we arrived was in marked contrast to the news reports.

### 1244. [contribute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/contribute) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒntrɪbjuːt/` · **US:** `/kənˈtrɪbjuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontribute__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontribute__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मदद करना, भाग देना, सहयोग देना
- **Examples:**
  - Do you wish to contribute?
  - contribute to/towards something Would you like to contribute to our collection?
  - to contribute to your pension
  - contribute something Check with your financial planner to make sure that you are contributing the correct amounts.

### 1245. [contribution](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/contribution) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌkɒntrɪˈbjuːʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌkɑːntrɪˈbjuːʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontribution__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontribution__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सहायता, चंदा, लेख
- **Examples:**
  - All contributions will be gratefully received.
  - We rely entirely on voluntary contributions.
  - campaign contributions
  - contribution to something to make a substantial contribution to charity

### 1246. [conventional](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/conventional) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈvenʃənl/` · **US:** `/kənˈvenʃənl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconv/xconventional__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconv/xconventional__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पारम्परिक, परमाणु रहित, रूढ़िगत/परम्परागत
- **Examples:**
  - conventional behaviour/morality
  - She's very conventional in her views.

### 1247. [convert](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/convert_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈvɜːt/` · **US:** `/kənˈvɜːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconv/xconvert__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconv/xconvert__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बदलना, परिवर्तित  होना, परिवर्तित होना
- **Examples:**
  - convert something They took just nine months to convert the building.
  - You need to ensure that you've converted the data properly.
  - The pub is a converted warehouse.
  - convert something into something The hotel is going to be converted into a nursing home.

### 1248. [convey](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/convey) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈveɪ/` · **US:** `/kənˈveɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconv/xconvey__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconv/xconvey__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रकट करना, ले  जाना, पहुँचाना
- **Examples:**
  - convey something Colours like red convey a sense of energy and strength.
  - convey something to somebody (formal) Please convey my apologies to your wife.
  - convey how, what, etc… He tried desperately to convey how urgent the situation was.
  - convey that… She did not wish to convey that they were all at fault.

### 1249. [convince](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/convince) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈvɪns/` · **US:** `/kənˈvɪns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconv/xconvince__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconv/xconvince__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दृढ़ मत होना, विश्वास  दिलाना, समझा देना
- **Examples:**
  - convince somebody/yourself Are the prime minister's assurances enough to convince the public?
  - I'd be very surprised if this argument convinces anybody.
  - to try/manage/fail to convince somebody/yourself
  - She has managed to convince even the sceptics.

### 1250. [cope](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/cope_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kəʊp/` · **US:** `/kəʊp/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cop/cope_/cope__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cop/cope_/cope__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सामना करना, पूराअना, सम्हालना
- **Examples:**
  - I got to the stage where I wasn't coping any more.
  - cope with something He wasn't able to cope with the stresses and strains of the job.
  - Desert plants are adapted to cope with extreme heat.

### 1251. [core](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/core_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/kɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cor/core_/core__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cor/core_/core__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बीजकोष, किसी चीज का मुख्य भाग, सब से महत्वपूर्ण भागा
- **Examples:**
  - core subjects (= subjects that all the students have to study) such as English and mathematics
  - the core curriculum
  - We need to concentrate on our core business.
  - The government's proposed solution fails to address the core issue.

### 1252. [corporate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/corporate) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɔːpərət/` · **US:** `/ˈkɔːrpərət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cor/corpo/corporate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cor/corpo/corporate__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निगम से  सम्बन्धित, संगठित, निगम से सम्बन्धित
- **Examples:**
  - corporate finance/profits/tax
  - corporate executives/clients/sponsors
  - corporate identity (= the image of a company, which all its members share)
  - corporate hospitality (= when companies entertain customers to help develop good business relationships)

### 1253. [corporation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/corporation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌkɔːpəˈreɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌkɔːrpəˈreɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cor/corpo/corporation__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cor/corpo/corporation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संस्था, निकाय, महापालिका
- **Examples:**
  - multinational corporations
  - the Chrysler corporation

### 1254. [correct](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/correct_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kəˈrekt/` · **US:** `/kəˈrekt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cor/corre/correct__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cor/corre/correct__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सही
- **Examples:**
  - Do you have the correct time?
  - the correct answer
  - When asked to pick the correct meaning from four alternatives, students consistently chose the correct one.
  - Please check that these details are correct.

### 1255. [correspond](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/correspond) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌkɒrəˈspɒnd/` · **US:** `/ˌkɔːrəˈspɑːnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cor/corre/correspond__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cor/corre/correspond__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** एकमत होना, मेल खाना, मेल  खाना
- **Examples:**
  - Your account and hers do not correspond.
  - correspond with something Your account of events does not correspond with hers.
  - correspond to something The written record of the conversation doesn't correspond to (= is different from) what was actually said.

### 1256. [corridor](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/corridor) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒrɪdɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈkɔːrɪdɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cor/corri/corridor__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cor/corri/corridor__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सीमांत  गलियारा, गलियारा, सीमांत गलियारा
- **Examples:**
  - His room is along the corridor.

### 1257. [council](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/council) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkaʊnsl/` · **US:** `/ˈkaʊnsl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cou/counc/council__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cou/counc/council__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** परिषद, सभा, समिति

  - a town council

  - He won a seat on the council in a by-election.

- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/counsel)

### 1259. [count](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/count_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kaʊnt/` · **US:** `/kaʊnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cou/count/count__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cou/count/count__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अपराध, गणना/गिनती, संख्या
- **Examples:**

### 1260. [counter](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/counter_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkaʊntə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈkaʊntər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cou/count/counter__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cou/count/counter__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गिनने का यंत्र, काउंटर, गणक

### 1261. [county](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/county_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkaʊnti/` · **US:** `/ˈkaʊnti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cou/count/county__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cou/count/county__us_2.mp3)

  - the southern/northern counties

### 1262. [courage](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/courage) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkʌrɪdʒ/` · **US:** `/ˈkɜːrɪdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cou/coura/courage__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cou/coura/courage__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** साहस, वीरता, धैर्य

  - He showed great courage and determination.
  - moral/physical courage
  - courage to do something They had the courage to stand up and fight for what they believed in.
  - He couldn't find the courage to tell his daughters what had happened.

### 1263. [crack](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/crack_2) *(noun)*

### 1265. [crash](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/crash_1) *(noun)*

### 1266. [crazy](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/crazy_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkreɪzi/` · **US:** `/ˈkreɪzi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cra/crazy/crazy__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cra/crazy/crazy__us_2.mp3)

  - What a crazy idea!

### 1267. [cream](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/cream_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kriːm/` · **US:** `/kriːm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cre/cream/cream__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cre/cream/cream__us_1.mp3)

### 1268. [creature](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/creature) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkriːtʃə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈkriːtʃər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cre/creat/creature__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cre/creat/creature__us_2.mp3)

### 1269. [credit](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/credit_1) *(noun)*

### 1270. [crew](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/crew_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kruː/` · **US:** `/kruː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cre/crew_/crew__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cre/crew_/crew__us_1.mp3)

  - The crew of the plane instructed everyone to remain seated.

### 1271. [crisis](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/crisis_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkraɪsɪs/` · **US:** `/ˈkraɪsɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cri/crisi/crisis__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cri/crisi/crisis__us_4.mp3)

  - a humanitarian crisis created by war and drought

### 1272. [criterion](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/criterion) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kraɪˈtɪəriən/` · **US:** `/kraɪˈtɪriən/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cri/crite/criterion__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cri/crite/criterion__us_2.mp3)

  - The main criterion is value for money.
  - She failed to meet the strict selection criteria.
  - What criteria are used for assessing a student's ability?
  - criterion for something The criteria for inclusion in the book are set out clearly in the preface.

### 1273. [critical](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/critical) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkrɪtɪkl/` · **US:** `/ˈkrɪtɪkl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cri/criti/critical__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cri/criti/critical__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ठीक, शोचनीय, सूक्ष्म
- **Examples:**
  - You should just ignore any critical comments.
  - The supervisor is always very critical.
  - critical of somebody/something Some parents are highly critical of the school.

### 1274. [criticism](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/criticism) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkrɪtɪsɪzəm/` · **US:** `/ˈkrɪtɪsɪzəm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cri/criti/criticism__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cri/criti/criticism__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समीक्षा, आलोचना/समीक्षा
- **Examples:**
  - to draw/face/receive criticism
  - The plan has attracted criticism from consumer groups.
  - She sought to deflect criticism by blaming her family.
  - People in public life must always be open to criticism (= willing to accept being criticized).

### 1275. [criticize](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/criticize) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkrɪtɪsaɪz/` · **US:** `/ˈkrɪtɪsaɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cri/criti/criticize__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cri/criti/criticize__us_3.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - All you ever do is criticize!
  - criticize somebody/something The decision was harshly criticized by environmental groups.
  - a heavily criticized plan
  - Journalists who strongly criticize the government can be heavily fined or imprisoned.

### 1276. [crop](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/crop_1) *(noun)*

### 1277. [cross](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/cross_2) *(noun)*

### 1278. [crowd](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/crowd_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kraʊd/` · **US:** `/kraʊd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cro/crowd/crowd__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cro/crowd/crowd__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/kraɪ/` · **US:** `/kraɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cry/cry__/cry__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cry/cry__/cry__us_1.mp3)

### 1291. [darkness](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/darkness) *(noun)*
