- **Examples:**
  - an art/antique dealer
  - dealer in something He's a dealer in second-hand cars.

### 1295. [declare](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/declare) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈkleə(r)/` · **US:** `/dɪˈkler/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dec/decla/declare__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dec/decla/declare__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बताना, प्रकट करना, कहना
- **Examples:**
  - declare something The government has declared a state of emergency.
  - Germany declared war on France on 1 August 1914.
  - The government has declared war on (= officially stated its intention to stop) illiteracy.
  - He will probably have to declare bankruptcy.

### 1296. [decline](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/decline_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈklaɪn/` · **US:** `/dɪˈklaɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dec/decli/decline__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dec/decli/decline__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पतन, ह्रास, पतन/क्षय
- **Examples:**
  - decline in something There has been a 5 per cent decline in student numbers.
  - decline of something the decline of seabird populations
  - a sharp/steep/rapid decline
  - a steady/gradual decline

### 1297. [decorate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/decorate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdekəreɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈdekəreɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dec/decor/decorate__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dec/decor/decorate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पदक प्रदान करना, सजाना, दीवारी कागज़  लगाना
- **Examples:**
  - decorate something with something They decorated the room with flowers and balloons.
  - decorate something The cake was decorated to look like a car.
  - The family gets together on Christmas Eve to decorate the tree.

### 1298. [decrease](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/decrease_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdiːkriːs/` · **US:** `/ˈdiːkriːs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dec/decre/decrease__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dec/decre/decrease__us_3_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कमी, घटती
- **Examples:**
  - Sales for May show a decrease compared with the same month last year.
  - decrease in something There has been some decrease in military spending this year.
  - a decrease in the number/level/rate of something
  - There has been a dramatic decrease in activity among our kids.

### 1299. [dedicate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/dedicate)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/dedicate)
- **Meaning:** समर्पित  करना, समर्पित करना

### 1300. [defeat](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/defeat_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈfiːt/` · **US:** `/dɪˈfiːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/def/defea/defeat__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/def/defea/defeat__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पराजय, हार, हारअ
- **Examples:**
  - The party faces defeat in the election.
  - They suffered a narrow defeat in the final.
  - a heavy/humiliating/crushing defeat
  - The world champion has only had two defeats in 20 fights.

## Words 1,301 – 1,400

### 1301. [defend](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/defend) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈfend/` · **US:** `/dɪˈfend/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/def/defen/defend__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/def/defen/defend__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** चलाना, बचाना, प्रतिवाद  करना
- **Examples:**
  - defend somebody/something The role of the military is to defend the country.
  - Troops have been sent to defend the borders.
  - defend somebody/something against somebody/something The male birds defend their territory against other males.
  - defend somebody/something from somebody/something The cannons once defended the city from attack by sea.

### 1302. [defendant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/defendant)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/defendant)
- **Meaning:** प्रतिवादी

### 1303. [define](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/define) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈfaɪn/` · **US:** `/dɪˈfaɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/def/defin/define__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/def/defin/define__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बताना, सीमांकन करना, स्पष्ट करना
- **Examples:**
  - define something The term ‘normal’ is difficult to define.
  - define something as something Life imprisonment is defined as 60 years under state law.

### 1304. [definitely](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/definitely) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdefɪnətli/` · **US:** `/ˈdefɪnətli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/def/defin/definitely__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/def/defin/definitely__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निश्चित रूप से, पक्का करना, स्पष्टता से
- **Examples:**
  - I definitely remember sending the letter.
  - ‘Was it what you expected?’ ‘Yes, definitely.’
  - ‘Do you plan to have children?’ ‘Definitely not!'
  - Some old people want help; others most definitely do not.

### 1305. [definition](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/definition) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌdefɪˈnɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌdefɪˈnɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/def/defin/definition__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/def/defin/definition__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रूपरेखा की  स्पष्टता, स्पष्ट, व्याख्या
- **Examples:**
  - The dictionary provides clear, simple definitions.
  - definition of something the dictionary definition of this term
  - a precise/strict definition of a word
  - We need a good working definition of ‘pollution’.

### 1306. [delay](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/delay_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈleɪ/` · **US:** `/dɪˈleɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/del/delay/delay__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/del/delay/delay__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विलम्ब, विलंब, देरी
- **Examples:**
  - Commuters will face long delays on the roads today.
  - to experience/suffer delays
  - to avoid/minimize further delays
  - Increased congestion and traffic delays are expected.

### 1307. [deliberate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/deliberate_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈlɪbərət/` · **US:** `/dɪˈlɪbərət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/del/delib/deliberate__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/del/delib/deliberate__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ध्यानपूर्वक
- **Examples:**
  - a deliberate act of vandalism
  - The speech was a deliberate attempt to embarrass the government.
  - a deliberate lie/deception

### 1308. [deliver](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/deliver) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈlɪvə(r)/` · **US:** `/dɪˈlɪvər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/del/deliv/deliver__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/del/deliv/deliver__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बताना, करना, प्रकट करना
- **Examples:**
  - deliver something I get my food delivered from the supermarket to save time.
  - deliver something to somebody/something Leaflets have been delivered to every household.
  - deliver (to somebody/something) We promise to deliver within 48 hours.
  - We deliver to our stores seven days a week.

### 1309. [delivery](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/delivery) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈlɪvəri/` · **US:** `/dɪˈlɪvəri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/del/deliv/delivery__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/del/deliv/delivery__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुपुर्दगी, प्रसव, प्रसूति
- **Examples:**
  - Allow 28 days for delivery.
  - We offer free delivery on orders over $200.
  - The service guarantees next day delivery.
  - on delivery Please pay for goods on delivery (= when you receive them).

### 1310. [demand](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/demand_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈmɑːnd/` · **US:** `/dɪˈmænd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dem/deman/demand__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dem/deman/demand__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मांग, चाह, आवश्यकता
- **Examples:**
  - demand for something a demand for higher pay
  - demand that… demands that the law on gun ownership should be changed
  - A federal judge rejected their demands.
  - The management had no intention of meeting union demands.

### 1311. [democracy](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/democracy) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈmɒkrəsi/` · **US:** `/dɪˈmɑːkrəsi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dem/democ/democracy__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dem/democ/democracy__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समानता, जनतन्ट्र, संचालन
- **Examples:**
  - parliamentary democracy
  - the principles of democracy

### 1312. [demonstrate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/demonstrate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdemənstreɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈdemənstreɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dem/demon/demonstrate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dem/demon/demonstrate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रमाणित करना, प्रदर्शन करना, दिखाना
- **Examples:**
  - demonstrate that… New research convincingly demonstrates that age-related memory loss is not inevitable.
  - demonstrate something These paintings demonstrate his extraordinary ability as a portrait painter.
  - The award demonstrated the importance that local people place on road safety.
  - This new anthology amply demonstrates the range and vitality of modern Scottish poetry.

### 1313. [denial](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/denial) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈnaɪəl/` · **US:** `/dɪˈnaɪəl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/den/denia/denial__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/den/denia/denial__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इंकार, निषेध, परित्याग
- **Examples:**
  - denial (of something) the prisoner’s repeated denials of the charges against him
  - The terrorists issued a denial of responsibility for the attack.
  - denial that… There was an official denial that there would be an election before the end of the year.
  - in denial She shook her head in denial.

### 1314. [deny](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/deny) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈnaɪ/` · **US:** `/dɪˈnaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/den/deny_/deny__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/den/deny_/deny__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रतिवाड करना, इन्कार करना, वंचित करना
- **Examples:**
  - deny something to deny an allegation/a charge/an accusation
  - He has denied any involvement in the incident.
  - The company strongly denies wrongdoing.
  - to vehemently/strenuously/categorically deny something

### 1315. [department](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/department) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈpɑːtmənt/` · **US:** `/dɪˈpɑːrtmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dep/depar/department__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dep/depar/department__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रदेश, विभाग, कार्यक्षेत्र
- **Examples:**

  - He is a lecturer in the department of psychology at Trinity College, Dublin.
  - the Treasury Department
  - a government/university, etc. department

### 1317. [dependent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/dependent_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈpendənt/` · **US:** `/dɪˈpendənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dep/depen/dependent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dep/depen/dependent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निर्भर
- **Examples:**
  - a woman with several dependent children
  - dependent on/upon somebody/something You can't be dependent on your parents all your life.
  - dependent on/upon somebody/something for something The festival is heavily dependent on sponsorship for its success.

### 1318. [depict](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/depict) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈpɪkt/` · **US:** `/dɪˈpɪkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dep/depic/depict__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dep/depic/depict__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दर्शाना, चित्रित  करना, चित्रित करना

  - depict somebody/something doing something The artist had depicted her lying on a bed.

### 1319. [deposit](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/deposit_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈpɒzɪt/` · **US:** `/dɪˈpɑːzɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dep/depos/deposit__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dep/depos/deposit__us_1.mp3)

### 1320. [depression](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/depression) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈpreʃn/` · **US:** `/dɪˈpreʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dep/depre/depression__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dep/depre/depression__us_1.mp3)

### 1321. [depth](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/depth) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/depθ/` · **US:** `/depθ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dep/depth/depth__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dep/depth/depth__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/ˈdepjuti/` · **US:** `/ˈdepjuti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dep/deput/deputy__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dep/deput/deputy__us_1.mp3)

### 1324. [description](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/description) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈskrɪpʃn/` · **US:** `/dɪˈskrɪpʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/des/descr/description__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/des/descr/description__us_1.mp3)

### 1325. [deserve](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/deserve) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈzɜːv/` · **US:** `/dɪˈzɜːrv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/des/deser/deserve__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/des/deser/deserve__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** योग्य होना, पात्र  होना, लायक होना
- **Examples:**

  - The report deserves careful consideration.
  - This idea deserves more attention.
  - One player in particular deserves a mention.

### 1326. [designer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/designer_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈzaɪnə(r)/` · **US:** `/dɪˈzaɪnər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/des/desig/designer__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/des/desig/designer__us_1.mp3)

### 1327. [desire](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/desire_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈzaɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/dɪˈzaɪər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/des/desir/desire__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/des/desir/desire__us_1.mp3)

  - (formal) I have no desire (= I do not want) to discuss the matter further.

### 1328. [desk](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/desk) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/desk/` · **US:** `/desk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/des/desk_/desk__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/des/desk_/desk__us_1.mp3)

### 1351. [disagree](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/disagree) *(verb)*

- **UK:** `/dɪˈskʌvəri/` · **US:** `/dɪˈskʌvəri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/disco/discovery__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/disco/discovery__us_1_rr.mp3)

### 1357. [discrimination](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/discrimination) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˌskrɪmɪˈneɪʃn/` · **US:** `/dɪˌskrɪmɪˈneɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/discr/discrimination__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/discr/discrimination__us_1.mp3)

  - discrimination in favour of somebody discrimination in favour of the young
  - discrimination by somebody They alleged discrimination by the authorities.

### 1358. [discussion](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/discussion) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈskʌʃn/` · **US:** `/dɪˈskʌʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/discu/discussion__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/discu/discussion__us_1.mp3)

  - We'll continue this discussion some other time.
  - discussion with somebody (about somebody/something) We had a discussion with them about the differences between Britain and the US.
  - discussion about somebody/something A lively discussion followed about whether he should be allowed to join the club.

### 1359. [dismiss](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/dismiss) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪsˈmɪs/` · **US:** `/dɪsˈmɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/dismi/dismiss__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/dismi/dismiss__us_3.mp3)

  - I think we can safely dismiss their objections.

### 1361. [dispute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/dispute_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdɪspjuːt/` · **US:** `/ˈdɪspjuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/dispu/dispute__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/dispu/dispute__us_3.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - industrial/pay disputes
  - dispute between A and B a dispute between the two countries about the border
  - dispute over/about something the latest dispute over fishing rights
  - in dispute with somebody/something The union is in dispute with management over working hours.

### 1362. [distinct](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/distinct) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈstɪŋkt/` · **US:** `/dɪˈstɪŋkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/disti/distinct__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/disti/distinct__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुस्पष्ट, स्पष्ट, अलग अलग
- **Examples:**
  - There was a distinct smell of gas.
  - His voice was quiet but every word was distinct.

### 1363. [distinguish](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/distinguish) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃ/` · **US:** `/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/disti/distinguish__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/disti/distinguish__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अलग करना, पहचानना, पहचान्ना
- **Examples:**
  - distinguish between A and B At what age are children able to distinguish between right and wrong?
  - English law clearly distinguishes between murder and manslaughter.
  - distinguish A from B It was hard to distinguish one twin from the other.
  - distinguish A and B Sometimes reality and fantasy are hard to distinguish.

### 1364. [distribute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/distribute) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdɪstrɪbjuːt/` · **US:** `/dɪˈstrɪbjuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/distr/distribute__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/distr/distribute__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** फैलना, वितरण करना, वितरण  करना
- **Examples:**
  - distribute something The leaflets have been widely distributed.
  - The newspaper is distributed free.
  - to distribute fliers/pamphlets/questionnaires
  - Viruses are often distributed via email.

### 1365. [distribution](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/distribution) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌdɪstrɪˈbjuːʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌdɪstrɪˈbjuːʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/distr/distribution__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/distr/distribution__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वितरण, विभाजन, वितरनअ
- **Examples:**
  - distribution of something They studied the geographical distribution of the disease.
  - a more equitable distribution of wealth
  - an unequal/uneven income distribution
  - the age distribution of the general population

### 1366. [district](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/district) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdɪstrɪkt/` · **US:** `/ˈdɪstrɪkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/distr/district__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/distr/district__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इलाका, जिला/मंडल, जनपद
- **Examples:**
  - the City of London’s financial district
  - Every city has its central business district.
  - The house is in a historic district.
  - Milan's most fashionable shopping district

### 1367. [diverse](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/diverse) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/daɪˈvɜːs/` · **US:** `/daɪˈvɜːrs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/div/diver/diverse__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/div/diver/diverse__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भिन्न, असमान, विविधतापूर्ण
- **Examples:**

  - My interests are very diverse.

### 1368. [diversity](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/diversity) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/daɪˈvɜːsəti/` · **US:** `/daɪˈvɜːrsəti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/div/diver/diversity__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/div/diver/diversity__us_1_rr.mp3)

  - the biological diversity of the rainforests
  - a great/wide/rich diversity of opinion

### 1369. [divide](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/divide_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈvaɪd/` · **US:** `/dɪˈvaɪd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/div/divid/divide__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/div/divid/divide__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जलसंभर
- **Examples:**
  - He offered advice on bridging cultural divides.
  - to straddle/cross a divide
  - The referendum revealed a deep urban-rural divide.
  - divide between A and B Will this deepen the divide between the country's rich and poor?

### 1370. [division](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/division) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈvɪʒn/` · **US:** `/dɪˈvɪʒn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/div/divis/division__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/div/divis/division__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वर्ग, हिस्सा, श्रेणी
- **Examples:**
  - The organism begins as a single cell and grows by cell division.
  - division of something We need to ensure a fair division of time and resources.
  - division (of something) between A and B the division of labour between the sexes
  - The living room is divided from a large terrace by glass walls so division between inside and outside is blurred.

### 1371. [divorce](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/divorce_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈvɔːs/` · **US:** `/dɪˈvɔːrs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/div/divor/divorce__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/div/divor/divorce__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पार्थक्य, विवाह विच्छेद, तलाक
- **Examples:**
  - The marriage ended in divorce in 1996.
  - an increase in the divorce rate (= the number of divorces in a year)
  - They have agreed to get a divorce.
  - Divorce proceedings (= the legal process of divorce) started today.

### 1372. [document](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/document_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdɒkjumənt/` · **US:** `/ˈdɑːkjumənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/doc/docum/document__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/doc/docum/document__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रपत्र, डक्युमेंट, लेख्य पत्र/दस्तावेज़
- **Examples:**
  - Please read and sign the attached document.
  - Please e-sign the document and email it back.
  - This is an important legal document.
  - The child is identified only by the initial ‘M’ in court documents.

### 1373. [domestic](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/domestic_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/dəˈmestɪk/` · **US:** `/dəˈmestɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dom/domes/domestic__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dom/domes/domestic__us_2.mp3)

### 1374. [dominant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/dominant) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdɒmɪnənt/` · **US:** `/ˈdɑːmɪnənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dom/domin/dominant__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dom/domin/dominant__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रबल/प्रधान

  - The dominant feature of the room was the large fireplace.

### 1375. [dominate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/dominate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdɒmɪneɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈdɑːmɪneɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dom/domin/dominate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dom/domin/dominate__us_1.mp3)

  - As a child he was dominated by his father.
  - professions that were once dominated by men

### 1376. [double](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/double_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdʌbl/` · **US:** `/ˈdʌbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dou/doubl/double__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dou/doubl/double__us_2.mp3)

### 1377. [doubt](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/doubt_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/daʊt/` · **US:** `/daʊt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dou/doubt/doubt__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dou/doubt/doubt__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/dʌst/` · **US:** `/dʌst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dus/dust_/dust__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dus/dust_/dust__us_1.mp3)

### 1386. [ease](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ease_1) *(noun)*

### 1390. [efficiency](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/efficiency) *(noun)*

  - element of something The story has all the elements of a soap opera.

### 1394. [eliminate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/eliminate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈlɪmɪneɪt/` · **US:** `/ɪˈlɪmɪneɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eli/elimi/eliminate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eli/elimi/eliminate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निकालना, हटाना, हटा देना
- **Examples:**
  - eliminate something Credit cards eliminate the need to carry a lot of cash.
  - eliminate something from something This diet claims to eliminate toxins from the body.

### 1395. [elite](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/elite_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈliːt/` · **US:** `/ɪˈliːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eli/elite/elite__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eli/elite/elite__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विशिष्ट  वर्ग, अभिजात वर्ग, विशिष्ट वर्ग
- **Examples:**
  - a member of the ruling/intellectual elite
  - Public opinion is influenced by the small elite who control the media.
  - In these countries, only the elite can afford an education for their children.

### 1396. [elsewhere](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/elsewhere) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌelsˈweə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˌelsˈwer/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/els/elsew/elsewhere__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/els/elsew/elsewhere__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** और कहीं, अन्यत्र, अनत
- **Examples:**
  - Dissatisfied customers will look elsewhere.
  - The site contains information not found elsewhere.
  - The answer to the problem must be sought elsewhere.
  - Our favourite restaurant was closed, so we had to go elsewhere.

### 1397. [emerge](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/emerge) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈmɜːdʒ/` · **US:** `/ɪˈmɜːrdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eme/emerg/emerge__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eme/emerg/emerge__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रकट होना, निकलना, प्रकट  होना
- **Examples:**
  - The crabs emerge at low tide to look for food.
  - emerge from something She finally emerged from her room at noon.
  - The swimmer emerged from the lake.
  - He emerged from the shadows.

### 1398. [emergency](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/emergency) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈmɜːdʒənsi/` · **US:** `/ɪˈmɜːrdʒənsi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eme/emerg/emergency__gb_5.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eme/emerg/emergency__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** एमर्जेंसी, आपात  काल, आपात काल
- **Examples:**
  - This is a medical emergency needing urgent treatment with antibiotics.
  - The government has declared a state of emergency following the earthquake.
  - In times of national emergency, the usual rules may not apply.
  - in an emergency This door should only be used in an emergency.

### 1399. [emission](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/emission) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈmɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪˈmɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/emi/emiss/emission__gb_5.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/emi/emiss/emission__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्राव, उत्सर्जन
- **Examples:**
  - the emission of carbon dioxide into the atmosphere
  - emission controls

### 1400. [emotion](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/emotion) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈməʊʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪˈməʊʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/emo/emoti/emotion__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/emo/emoti/emotion__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भावना, उमङ्ग, जज़्बा
- **Examples:**
  - to show/express your emotions
  - They expressed mixed emotions at the news.
  - Counselling can teach people to handle negative emotions such as fear and anger.
  - Fear is a normal human emotion.

## Words 1,401 – 1,500

### 1401. [emotional](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/emotional) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈməʊʃənl/` · **US:** `/ɪˈməʊʃənl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/emo/emoti/emotional__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/emo/emoti/emotional__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उत्तेजित, भावुक, भावात्मक
- **Examples:**
  - emotional problems/stress
  - The emotional impact of the film is powerful and lasting.
  - He clearly feels an emotional connection to the place.
  - a child’s emotional and intellectual development

### 1402. [emphasis](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/emphasis) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈemfəsɪs/` · **US:** `/ˈemfəsɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/emp/empha/emphasis__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/emp/empha/emphasis__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बल, महत्व, जोर
- **Examples:**
  - emphasis on/upon something Since the elections there has been a greater emphasis on education.
  - to put/lay/place emphasis on something
  - to put particular/special/strong emphasis on something
  - Increased emphasis is now being placed on corporate image.

### 1403. [emphasize](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/emphasize) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈemfəsaɪz/` · **US:** `/ˈemfəsaɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/emp/empha/emphasize__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/emp/empha/emphasize__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** महत्त्व  देना, महत्त्व देना, बल देना
- **Examples:**
  - emphasize something His speech emphasized the importance of attracting industry to the town.
  - The report emphasizes the need for economic stability.
  - She emphasized the point in an interview at the weekend.
  - emphasize that… She emphasized that their plan would mean sacrifices and hard work.

### 1404. [employ](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/employ_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈplɔɪ/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈplɔɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/emp/emplo/employ__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/emp/emplo/employ__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नियुक्त करना, नौकर  रखना, काम में लगाना
- **Examples:**
  - employ somebody How many people does the company employ?
  - His company currently employs 135 workers in total.
  - We employ 16 full-time staff.
  - employ somebody as something For the past three years he has been employed as a firefighter.

### 1405. [employee](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/employee) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈplɔɪiː/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈplɔɪiː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/emp/emplo/employee__gb_5.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/emp/emplo/employee__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कर्मचारी
- **Examples:**
  - The firm has over 500 employees.
  - They have eight full-time and two part-time employees.
  - government/state/federal employees
  - He is in charge of hiring and firing employees.

### 1406. [employer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/employer) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈplɔɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈplɔɪər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/emp/emplo/employer__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/emp/emplo/employer__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मालिक, प्रवर्तक
- **Examples:**
  - They're very good employers (= they treat the people that work for them well).
  - They're one of the largest employers in the area.
  - These skills are highly regarded by potential employers.
  - A prospective employer will want to see your portfolio.

### 1407. [employment](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/employment) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈplɔɪmənt/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈplɔɪmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/emp/emplo/employment__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/emp/emplo/employment__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** काम, आजीविका, नौकरी
- **Examples:**
  - full-time/part-time employment
  - people seeking employment
  - They are finding it more and more difficult to find employment.
  - The steelworks provided employment for thousands of people.

### 1408. [enable](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/enable) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈneɪbl/` · **US:** `/ɪˈneɪbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ena/enabl/enable__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ena/enabl/enable__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्राप्त करना, अधिकार  देना, अधिकार देना
- **Examples:**
  - This new programme will enable older people to study at college.
  - This approach enables the company to focus on its core business.
  - Insulin enables the body to use and store sugar.
  - The tool enables users to test their computer's exposure to a wide range of online threats.

### 1409. [encounter](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/encounter_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈkaʊntə(r)/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈkaʊntər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/enc/encou/encounter__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/enc/encou/encounter__us_5.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मुठभेड़, लड़ाई, आकस्मिक  भेंट
- **Examples:**
  - a chance encounter
  - encounter with somebody/something Three of them were killed in the subsequent encounter with the police.
  - Flaubert was her first encounter with French literature.
  - encounter between A and B The story describes the extraordinary encounter between a man and a dolphin.

### 1410. [encourage](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/encourage) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈkʌrɪdʒ/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/enc/encou/encourage__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/enc/encou/encourage__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रोत्साहित  करना, उत्तेजित करना, उकसाना
- **Examples:**
  - encourage somebody We were greatly encouraged by the positive response of the public.
  - She encouraged many young writers and artists.
  - encourage somebody in something My parents have always encouraged me in my choice of career.
  - + speech ‘You're doing fine,’ he encouraged her.

### 1411. [endure](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/endure) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈdjʊə(r)/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈdʊr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/end/endur/endure__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/end/endur/endure__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सहना, सहन करना, टिके रहना
- **Examples:**
  - endure something They had to endure a long wait before the case came to trial.
  - She could not endure the thought of parting.
  - The pain was almost too great to endure.
  - (formal) a love that endures all things and never fails

### 1412. [enemy](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/enemy) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈenəmi/` · **US:** `/ˈenəmi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ene/enemy/enemy__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ene/enemy/enemy__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दुश्मन, शत्रु, अरि
- **Examples:**
  - She didn't have an enemy in the world.
  - He has a lot of enemies in the company.
  - After just one day, she had already made an enemy of her manager.
  - They united in the face of a common enemy.

### 1413. [enforce](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/enforce) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈfɔːs/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈfɔːrs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/enf/enfor/enforce__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/enf/enfor/enforce__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लागू  करना, लागू करना
- **Examples:**
  - enforce something It's the job of the police to enforce the law.
  - The legislation will be difficult to enforce.
  - United Nations troops enforced a ceasefire in the area.
  - enforce something against/on somebody/something (formal or law) The creditor should be at liberty to enforce his rights against the company's property.

### 1414. [engage](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/engage) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈɡeɪdʒ/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈɡeɪdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eng/engag/engage__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eng/engag/engage__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नियुक्त करना, आकर्षित करना, आक्रमण करना
- **Examples:**
  - It is a movie that engages both the mind and the eye.
  - Their pleas failed to engage any sympathy.
  - to engage the public/audience/viewer/reader
  - It was difficult to engage the students at first.

### 1415. [engine](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/engine) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈendʒɪn/` · **US:** `/ˈendʒɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eng/engin/engine__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eng/engin/engine__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मशीन, इंजन, यन्त्र
- **Examples:**
  - a diesel/petrol engine
  - I got in the car and started the engine.
  - My car had to have a new engine.
  - The engine runs on diesel.

### 1416. [engineer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/engineer_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌendʒɪˈnɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˌendʒɪˈnɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eng/engin/engineer__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eng/engin/engineer__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अभियन्ता, यांट्रिक, इंजीनियर
- **Examples:**
  - We worked with a team of scientists and engineers.
  - You need the advice of a qualified engineer.
  - engineers who designed and built advanced military aircraft

### 1417. [engineering](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/engineering) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌendʒɪˈnɪərɪŋ/` · **US:** `/ˌendʒɪˈnɪrɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eng/engin/engineering__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eng/engin/engineering__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अभियान्त्रिकी, कल बनाने की विद्या, अभियांत्रिकी
- **Examples:**
  - The bridge is a triumph of modern engineering.
  - an engineering firm/company
  - Train services on Sunday will be restricted because of engineering works.

### 1418. [enhance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/enhance) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈhɑːns/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈhæns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/enh/enhan/enhance__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/enh/enhan/enhance__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बढ़ाना
- **Examples:**
  - This is an opportunity to enhance the reputation of the company.
  - things that can significantly enhance the quality of your life
  - The runner says he's never used steroids to enhance his performance.
  - The new initiative will enhance our ability to respond to threats abroad.

### 1419. [enormous](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/enormous) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈnɔːməs/` · **US:** `/ɪˈnɔːrməs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eno/enorm/enormous__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eno/enorm/enormous__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बहुत, विशाल, बड़ा
- **Examples:**
  - an enormous house/dog
  - an enormous amount of time
  - Current processors can perform an enormous number of operations per second.
  - Universities are under enormous pressure financially.

### 1420. [ensure](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ensure) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈʃɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈʃʊr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ens/ensur/ensure__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ens/ensur/ensure__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुनिश्चित करना, सुनिश्चित  करना, आश्वस्त करना
- **Examples:**
  - ensure something We are working to ensure the safety of people in the city.
  - The book ensured his success.
  - We must take steps now to ensure the survival of these animals.
  - ensure somebody something Victory ensured them a place in the final.

### 1421. [enterprise](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/enterprise) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈentəpraɪz/` · **US:** `/ˈentərpraɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ent/enter/enterprise__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ent/enter/enterprise__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उद्योग, उद्यम, साहसिक कार्य
- **Examples:**
  - He is in charge of an enterprise with a turnover of $26 billion.
  - state-owned/public enterprises
  - The grant is available to small and medium-sized enterprises.

### 1422. [entertain](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/entertain) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌentəˈteɪn/` · **US:** `/ˌentərˈteɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ent/enter/entertain__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ent/enter/entertain__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुनना, विचार करना, विचार  करना
- **Examples:**
  - The aim of the series is both to entertain and inform.
  - entertain somebody/yourself Afterwards live bands will entertain the crowd.
  - Parents can relax while the kids entertain themselves in the outdoor playground.
  - entertain somebody/yourself with something He entertained us for hours with his stories and jokes.

### 1423. [entertainment](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/entertainment) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌentəˈteɪnmənt/` · **US:** `/ˌentərˈteɪnmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ent/enter/entertainment__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ent/enter/entertainment__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तमाशा, मनोरंजन, मौज
- **Examples:**
  - radio, television and other forms of entertainment
  - There will be live entertainment at the party.
  - It was typical family entertainment.
  - It was not something you would watch for light entertainment.

### 1424. [enthusiastic](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/enthusiastic) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˌθjuːziˈæstɪk/` · **US:** `/ɪnˌθuːziˈæstɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ent/enthu/enthusiastic__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ent/enthu/enthusiastic__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उत्साहपूर्ण, उत्साही, उत्साहयुक्त
- **Examples:**
  - an enthusiastic supporter
  - enthusiastic applause
  - They gave her an enthusiastic reception.
  - enthusiastic about somebody/something You don't sound very enthusiastic about the idea.

### 1425. [entitle](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/entitle) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈtaɪtl/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈtaɪtl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ent/entit/entitle__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ent/entit/entitle__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उपाधि देना, अधिकार देना, हकदार होना
- **Examples:**
  - be entitled to something You will be entitled to your pension when you reach 65.
  - Everyone's entitled to their own opinion.
  - entitle somebody to do something This ticket does not entitle you to travel first class.

### 1426. [entity](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/entity) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈentəti/` · **US:** `/ˈentəti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ent/entit/entity__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ent/entit/entity__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हस्तीस्थिति, तत्त्व, सत्व
- **Examples:**
  - The unit has become part of a larger department and no longer exists as a separate entity.
  - These countries can no longer be viewed as a single entity.
  - Hip hop is a major commercial entity now (= it is a business, not just a type of music).
  - A corporation is a separate legal entity and it shields you from personal liability for its debts.

### 1427. [entrance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/entrance1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈentrəns/` · **US:** `/ˈentrəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ent/entra/entrance__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ent/entra/entrance__us_7.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दाखिला, द्वार, पैठ
- **Examples:**
  - the front/back/side entrance of the house
  - A separate entrance leads to the garden.
  - At last we reached the entrance of the cave.
  - entrance to something Protesters blocked the entrance to the building.

### 1428. [entry](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/entry) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈentri/` · **US:** `/ˈentri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ent/entry/entry__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ent/entry/entry__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दहलीज, प्रवेशाधिकार, द्वार
- **Examples:**
  - She made her entry to the sound of thunderous applause.
  - The children were surprised by the sudden entry of their teacher.
  - They block the entry of the HIV virus into the cells.
  - entry into something How did the thieves gain entry into the building?

### 1429. [episode](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/episode) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈepɪsəʊd/` · **US:** `/ˈepɪsəʊd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/epi/episo/episode__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/epi/episo/episode__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वृतान्त, घटनाअना, कड़ी
- **Examples:**
  - The next episode has not yet been filmed.
  - I watched a few episodes of seasons one and two.
  - The soap opera is ending after 175 episodes.
  - episode in something I only saw the first episode in the series

### 1430. [equal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/equal_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈiːkwəl/` · **US:** `/ˈiːkwəl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/equ/equal/equal__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/equ/equal/equal__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समान
- **Examples:**
  - There is an equal number of boys and girls in the class.
  - Each side will put in an equal amount of money.
  - Cut it into four equal parts.
  - equal in something The two countries are roughly equal in size.

### 1431. [equally](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/equally) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈiːkwəli/` · **US:** `/ˈiːkwəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/equ/equal/equally__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/equ/equal/equally__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समान रूप से, समान रूप  से, साथ ही साथ
- **Examples:**
  - Diet and exercise are equally important.
  - equally good/impressive/effective
  - This job could be done equally well by a computer.
  - Everyone should be treated equally.

### 1432. [equipment](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/equipment) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈkwɪpmənt/` · **US:** `/ɪˈkwɪpmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/equ/equip/equipment__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/equ/equip/equipment__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उपकरण, तैयारी
- **Examples:**
  - a useful piece of equipment for the kitchen
  - Take care when using old electrical equipment.
  - electronic/communications/computer equipment
  - military equipment and supplies

### 1433. [equivalent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/equivalent_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈkwɪvələnt/` · **US:** `/ɪˈkwɪvələnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/equ/equiv/equivalent__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/equ/equiv/equivalent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बराबर, समतुल्य, तुल्य
- **Examples:**
  - 250 grams or an equivalent amount in ounces
  - the price we would pay elsewhere for a broadly equivalent house
  - equivalent to something Eight kilometres is roughly equivalent to five miles.
  - The new regulation was seen as equivalent to censorship.

### 1434. [era](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/era) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪərə/` · **US:** `/ˈerə/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/era/era__/era__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/era/era__/era__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कालअ, युग, युग/संवत कल्प
- **Examples:**
  - the Victorian/modern/post-war era
  - When she left the firm, it was the end of an era (= things were different after that).
  - a new era of peace
  - This is the start of a new era of peace and prosperity.

### 1435. [error](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/error) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈerə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈerər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/err/error/error__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/err/error/error__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भूल, गलती
- **Examples:**
  - spelling/typographical/grammatical errors
  - The report contains some factual errors that must be corrected.
  - They committed the fatal error of misjudging the weather conditions.
  - error in something There are too many errors in your work.

### 1436. [establishment](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/establishment) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈstæblɪʃmənt/` · **US:** `/ɪˈstæblɪʃmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/est/estab/establishment__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/est/estab/establishment__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्थान, संस्था, नींव
- **Examples:**
  - The visa is for foreign nationals wishing to study at a university, college or similar educational establishment.
  - She's now running a small government research establishment.
  - The hotel is a comfortable and well-run establishment.

### 1437. [estate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/estate) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈsteɪt/` · **US:** `/ɪˈsteɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/est/estat/estate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/est/estat/estate__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भूमि, रियासत, जागीर
- **Examples:**
  - on an estate She lives in a tower block on an estate in London.

### 1438. [estimate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/estimate_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈestɪmət/` · **US:** `/ˈestɪmət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/est/estim/estimate__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/est/estim/estimate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आकलन, मूल्य का अनुमान
- **Examples:**
  - to provide/obtain an estimate
  - estimate of something I can give you a rough estimate of the amount of wood you will need.
  - What is the latest estimate of the number of chimpanzees in the wild?
  - official government estimates of traffic growth over the next decade

### 1439. [ethics](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ethics)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ethics)
- **Meaning:** नीति, नीतिशास्त्र

### 1440. [evaluate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/evaluate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈvæljueɪt/` · **US:** `/ɪˈvæljueɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eva/evalu/evaluate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eva/evalu/evaluate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मूल्यांकन करना, मूल्यांकन  करना
- **Examples:**
  - evaluate something The trial will evaluate the effectiveness of the different drugs.
  - Researchers evaluated teaching performance by comparing students' exam results.
  - evaluate how, whether, etc… The study will critically evaluate whether this technology is useful.
  - We need to evaluate how well the policy is working.

### 1441. [evaluation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/evaluation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˌvæljuˈeɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪˌvæljuˈeɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eva/evalu/evaluation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eva/evalu/evaluation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मूल्यांकन, आंकलन
- **Examples:**
  - an evaluation of the healthcare system
  - The technique is not widely practised and requires further evaluation.
  - The discussion includes a critical evaluation of the documentary sources.

### 1442. [eventually](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/eventually) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈventʃuəli/` · **US:** `/ɪˈventʃuəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eve/event/eventually__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eve/event/eventually__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अंत में, आखिरकार, अंततोगत्वा
- **Examples:**
  - Our flight eventually left five hours late.
  - I'll get round to mending it eventually.
  - She hopes eventually to attend medical school and become a doctor.

### 1443. [evolution](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/evolution) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌevəˈluːʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌevəˈluːʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/evo/evolu/evolution__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/evo/evolu/evolution__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विकासक्रम, विकास, क्रमिक  विकास
- **Examples:**
  - the evolution of the human species
  - Darwin’s theory of evolution
  - Finally it was legal to teach evolution everywhere in America.

### 1444. [evolve](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/evolve) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈvɒlv/` · **US:** `/ɪˈvɑːlv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/evo/evolv/evolve__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/evo/evolv/evolve__us_5.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विकसित होना, विकसित करना, विकसित  होना
- **Examples:**
  - evolve (from something) (into something) The idea evolved from a drawing I discovered in the attic.
  - The company has evolved into a major chemical manufacturer.
  - evolve something (from something) Each school must evolve its own way of working.

### 1445. [exact](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/exact_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪɡˈzækt/` · **US:** `/ɪɡˈzækt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exa/exact/exact__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exa/exact/exact__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निर्दोष, ठीक, शुद्ध
- **Examples:**
  - The new palace is an exact replica of the original building.
  - It is an exact copy of the original document.
  - The model is exact in every detail.
  - For now they are keeping the exact date and location of the wedding a secret.

### 1446. [examine](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/examine) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪɡˈzæmɪn/` · **US:** `/ɪɡˈzæmɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exa/exami/examine__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exa/exami/examine__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समझना, परखना, पूछना
- **Examples:**
  - examine something This study sets out to examine in detail the possible effects of climate change.
  - Her new show critically examines the relationship between the arts and popular culture.
  - This important issue must be carefully examined.
  - Only recently have historians begun to examine the impact and influence of these changes.

### 1447. [exceed](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/exceed) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˈsiːd/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈsiːd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exc/excee/exceed__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exc/excee/exceed__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अधिक होना, पार कर जाना, अतिक्रमण करना
- **Examples:**
  - The price will not exceed £100.

### 1448. [excellence](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/excellence) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈeksələns/` · **US:** `/ˈeksələns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exc/excel/excellence__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exc/excel/excellence__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रतिष्ठा, विशिष्टता, श्रेष्ठता
- **Examples:**
  - a reputation for academic excellence
  - excellence in something The hospital is recognized as a centre of excellence in research and teaching.

### 1449. [excellent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/excellent) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈeksələnt/` · **US:** `/ˈeksələnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exc/excel/excellent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exc/excel/excellent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनोखा, उत्कृष्ट, अच्छा
- **Examples:**
  - an excellent book/article
  - excellent results/work
  - The meal looked and tasted excellent.
  - Both the food and the service were truly excellent.

### 1450. [exception](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/exception) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˈsepʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈsepʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exc/excep/exception__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exc/excep/exception__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अपवाद
- **Examples:**
  - Most of the buildings in the town are modern, but the church is an exception.
  - With very few exceptions, private schools get the best exam results.
  - Nobody had much money at the time and I was no exception.

### 1451. [excess](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/excess_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈekses/` · **US:** `/ˈekses/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exc/exces/excess__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exc/exces/excess__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बहुत अधिक, अधिशेष, अधिकता
- **Examples:**
  - Excess food is stored as fat.
  - Driving with excess alcohol in the blood is a serious offence.
  - The book is hard to obtain, because of excess demand following the author’s appearance on TV.

### 1452. [exchange](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/exchange_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪksˈtʃeɪndʒ/` · **US:** `/ɪksˈtʃeɪndʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exc/excha/exchange__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exc/excha/exchange__us_1.mp3)

  - an exchange of fire (= between enemy soldiers)

### 1454. [exclude](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/exclude) *(verb)*

- **UK:** `/ɪkˈskjuːs/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈskjuːs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exc/excus/excuse__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exc/excus/excuse__us_1.mp3)

### 1456. [execute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/execute) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈeksɪkjuːt/` · **US:** `/ˈeksɪkjuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exe/execu/execute__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exe/execu/execute__us_1.mp3)

### 1457. [execution](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/execution) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌeksɪˈkjuːʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌeksɪˈkjuːʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exe/execu/execution__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exe/execu/execution__us_1.mp3)

  - Over 200 executions were carried out last year.

### 1458. [exhibit](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/exhibit_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪɡˈzɪbɪt/` · **US:** `/ɪɡˈzɪbɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exh/exhib/exhibit__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exh/exhib/exhibit__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रदर्शन, प्रदर्शनी, सबूत
- **Examples:**

### 1459. [exhibition](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/exhibition) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌeksɪˈbɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌeksɪˈbɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exh/exhib/exhibition__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exh/exhib/exhibition__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रदर्शनी, प्रदरशनी, नुमाइश
- **Examples:**

  - to hold/organize an exhibition
  - an art exhibition
  - His first solo exhibition took place in Barcelona in 1925.

### 1460. [exist](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/exist) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪɡˈzɪst/` · **US:** `/ɪɡˈzɪst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exi/exist/exist__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exi/exist/exist__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, रहना, जीवित रहना
- **Examples:**
  - Does life exist on other planets?
  - Few of these monkeys still exist in the wild.
  - Major differences continue to exist between the two countries.
  - A solution has been found and the problem no longer exists.

### 1461. [existence](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/existence) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪɡˈzɪstəns/` · **US:** `/ɪɡˈzɪstəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exi/exist/existence__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exi/exist/existence__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सत्ता, जीवन, ज़िन्दगी
- **Examples:**
  - I was unaware of his existence until today.
  - to believe in the existence of God
  - the mystery of human existence
  - The continued existence of the industry depends on the investment of public money.

### 1462. [existing](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/existing)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/existing)
- **Meaning:** प्रचलित, वर्तमान, मौजूदा

### 1463. [expand](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/expand) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˈspænd/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈspænd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/expan/expand__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/expan/expand__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** फैलना, फैलाना, फैल जाना
- **Examples:**
  - Metals expand when they are heated.
  - Student numbers are expanding rapidly.
  - a greatly expanded version of his earlier book
  - A child's vocabulary expands through reading.

### 1464. [expansion](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/expansion) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˈspænʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈspænʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/expan/expansion__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/expan/expansion__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विस्तार, प्रसार
- **Examples:**
  - a period of rapid economic expansion
  - Despite the recession the company is confident of further expansion.
  - The expansion of higher education will continue.
  - The book is an expansion of a series of lectures given last year.

### 1465. [expectation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/expectation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌekspekˈteɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌekspekˈteɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/expec/expectation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/expec/expectation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उम्मीद, चाह, कफोत्सारक
- **Examples:**
  - expectation of something We are confident in our expectation of a full recovery.
  - We certainly had a reasonable expectation of success.
  - expectation about something the government's expectations about the economy
  - expectation that… There was a general expectation that he would win.

### 1466. [expense](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/expense) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˈspens/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈspens/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/expen/expense__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/expen/expense__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लागत, खर्च
- **Examples:**
  - The garden was transformed at great expense.
  - No expense was spared (= they spent as much money as was needed) to make the party a success.
  - He's arranged everything, no expense spared.
  - She always travels first-class regardless of expense.

### 1467. [expensive](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/expensive) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˈspensɪv/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈspensɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/expen/expensive__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/expen/expensive__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बहुमूल्य, क़ीमती, मह्ंअगा
- **Examples:**
  - an expensive car/restaurant/holiday
  - I can't afford it—it's too expensive.
  - Making the wrong decision could prove expensive.
  - That dress was an expensive mistake.

### 1468. [experiment](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/experiment_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˈsperɪmənt/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈsperɪmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/exper/experiment__gb_6.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/exper/experiment__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** परीक्षा, प्रयोग
- **Examples:**
  - to do/perform/conduct an experiment
  - laboratory experiments
  - The results of the experiment were inconclusive.
  - in an experiment The 30 subjects in each experiment were divided into two groups.

### 1469. [experimental](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/experimental) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˌsperɪˈmentl/` · **US:** `/ɪkˌsperɪˈmentl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/exper/experimental__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/exper/experimental__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रायोगिक
- **Examples:**
  - The school's experimental teaching methods include letting the children decide what to study.
  - experimental theatre/art/music
  - The equipment is still at the experimental stage.
  - Doctors stress that this kind of treatment is still experimental.

### 1470. [expertise](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/expertise) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌekspɜːˈtiːz/` · **US:** `/ˌekspɜːrˈtiːz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/exper/expertise__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/exper/expertise__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दक्षता
- **Examples:**
  - professional/scientific/technical, etc. expertise
  - We have the expertise to help you run your business.
  - It is difficult to find staff with the level of expertise required for this job.
  - expertise in something/in doing something They have considerable expertise in dealing with oil spills.

### 1471. [explanation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/explanation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌekspləˈneɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌekspləˈneɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/expla/explanation__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/expla/explanation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्पष्टीकरण, व्याख्या, सफ़ाई
- **Examples:**
  - The most likely explanation is that his plane was delayed.
  - It's the only explanation that makes any sense.
  - a plausible/an alternative explanation
  - to offer/provide an explanation

### 1472. [explicit](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/explicit) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˈsplɪsɪt/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈsplɪsɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/expli/explicit__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/expli/explicit__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुस्पष्ट, स्पष्ट, सुनिश्चित
- **Examples:**

  - The reasons for the decision should be made explicit.
  - She made some very explicit references to my personal life.

### 1473. [explore](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/explore) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˈsplɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈsplɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/explo/explore__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/explo/explore__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अन्वेषण  करना, खोज करना, अन्वेषण करना
- **Examples:**
  - As soon as we arrived on the island we were eager to explore.
  - explore something They explored the land to the south of the Murray River.
  - The city is best explored on foot.
  - Westerners did not set out to explore the world until the fifteenth century.

### 1474. [export](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/export_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈekspɔːt/` · **US:** `/ˈekspɔːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/expor/export__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/expor/export__us_2.mp3)
